# Bot API

> Source: [https://docs.aiogram.dev/en/latest/api/index.html](https://docs.aiogram.dev/en/latest/api/index.html)

**aiogram** now is fully support of [Telegram Bot API](https://core.telegram.org/bots/api)

All methods and types is fully autogenerated from Telegram Bot API docs by parser with code-generator.
